- **p**: Shortcut for "parallelize" — take the work in flight (or the named work), split off the parts that do not depend on each other, and run them at the same time so it finishes sooner. "p" splits what clearly splits, "pp" splits harder, "ppp" is maximum parallelism; "p1", "p2", "p3" are the same three levels. "ff" and "fff" are levels 2 and 3 too; a single "f" is the fast-mode toggle, not a level. Bare forms mean the same thing. ([danielmiessler/LifeOS](https://github.com/danielmiessler/LifeOS/blob/HEAD/LifeOS/install/commands/p.md))

- **p_s**: /p_s — portfolio snapshot. Three input modes — (a) read journal/positions.json (no args, default), (b) parse positions from an image attachment (a broker screenshot — works from Telegram or IDE), (c) inline positions paste in $ARGUMENTS. When invoked from a Telegram channel session, also replies via the Telegram reply tool with the report. Read-only — does not modify any file. Canonical caption is /p_s (short for portfolio-snapshot). ([shinbertrand-spec/claude1-swing-trading](https://github.com/shinbertrand-spec/claude1-swing-trading/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/p_s.md) · ★1)
- **p_s_onboard**: /p_s_onboard — onboard pre-framework positions into ledgered positions. Same input modes as /p_s (image attachment, inline --positions, or — for re-onboarding — journal/positions.json itself). Writes ledgers/positions/&lt;TICKER&gt;.yml + appends to journal/positions.json. After completion, the EOD sell-decision pipeline + check-positions.ps1 + news-research Scout pass all start picking these positions up. Direct-write — use deliberately. ([shinbertrand-spec/claude1-swing-trading](https://github.com/shinbertrand-spec/claude1-swing-trading/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/p_s_onboard.md) · ★1)
- **p_s_sync**: /p_s_sync — portfolio sync. Compares journal/positions.json against the live Tiger paper account (read-only). Surfaces drift — positions in Tiger not in journal, positions in journal not in Tiger, share-count / cost-basis mismatches, and (with --include-orders) any open Tiger orders without matching journal positions. Does NOT reconcile — the caller decides whether to onboard, close, or amend. Canonical caption is /p_s_sync (short for portfolio-sync). ([shinbertrand-spec/claude1-swing-trading](https://github.com/shinbertrand-spec/claude1-swing-trading/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/p_s_sync.md) · ★1)

- **palimpsest-archive**: Archive a completed Palimpsest article. This command finalizes an article by: 1. Copying `article.mdx` to `assets/archive/{article-id}-{version}.mdx` 2. Optionally cleaning up the `assets/articles/{article-id}/` working directory ([FourWindff/Palimpsest](https://github.com/FourWindff/Palimpsest/blob/HEAD/commands/palimpsest-archive.md))
- **palimpsest-writing**: Palimpsest is a file-driven writing system with live diff visualization. - **pan.cs**: Jak posouvat pohled v AutoCADu příkazem PP (PAN, zkratka P): kurzor ve tvaru ruky nebo kolečko myši, -PP o dva body a PP uvnitř jiného příkazu. Vyzkoušejte. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/pan.cs.md))
- **pan.de**: So arbeitet AutoCADs PAN (Alias P): die Ansicht mit dem Handcursor oder der Radtaste ziehen, -PAN über zwei Punkte, und PAN innerhalb eines anderen Befehls. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/pan.de.md))
- **pan.es**: Cómo funciona ENCUADRE (PAN, alias E) de AutoCAD en español: arrastra la vista con la mano o la rueda, -ENCUADRE por dos puntos y dentro de otro comando. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/pan.es.md))
- **pan.fr**: Panoramique avec PAN (alias P) d'AutoCAD en français : glissez la vue avec la main ou la molette, -PAN par deux points, PAN dans une commande. Essayez-le. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/pan.fr.md))
- **pan.hu**: Az AutoCAD TOL (PAN) parancsa: a nézet húzása kéz kurzorral vagy a görgővel, -TOL két ponttal, és TOL egy másik parancson belül. Ingyen, online. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/pan.hu.md))
- **pan.it**: Come funziona PAN (alias P) di AutoCAD in italiano: trascina la vista con il cursore a mano o con la rotellina, -PAN con due punti, e PAN dentro un comando. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/pan.it.md))
- **pan.ja**: AutoCAD の PAN[画面移動](P)の使い方を解説します。手のひらカーソルやホイール ボタンでビューをドラッグ、-PAN で 2 点を指定、コマンド実行中の PAN。ブラウザで無料で試せます。 ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/pan.ja.md))
- **pan.ko**: AutoCAD 초점이동(PAN, P) 사용법을 설명합니다: 손 모양 커서나 휠 버튼으로 뷰 끌기, 두 점으로 이동하는 -PAN, 다른 명령 도중의 PAN. 브라우저에서 무료로 해 볼 수 있습니다. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/pan.ko.md))
- **pan.pl**: Jak przesuwać widok w AutoCADzie poleceniem NFRAGM (PAN, skrót NF): kursor w kształcie dłoni lub kółko myszy, -NFRAGM o dwa punkty i NFRAGM w innym poleceniu. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/pan.pl.md))
- **pan.pt-BR**: Como funciona o PAN (P) do AutoCAD em português: arraste a vista com o cursor de mão ou o botão da roda, o -PAN por dois pontos e o PAN dentro de outro comando. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/pan.pt-BR.md))
- **pan.ru**: Как панорамировать в AutoCAD: команда ПАН (PAN) — вид перетаскивается курсором-рукой или колесиком мыши, -ПАН по двум точкам, ПАН внутри другой команды. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/pan.ru.md))
- **pan.zh-CN**: CAD 怎么平移视图：AutoCAD PAN（平移，快捷键 P）用手形光标或鼠标滚轮拖动视图，-PAN 按两点平移，以及在其他命令中透明平移。在浏览器中免费在线试用。 ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/pan.zh-CN.md))

- **panel:ai-feature**: Run three AI-layer agents as a panel — ai-llm (Claude API integration, streaming, voice pipeline), ai-prompt-engineer (system prompt design, structured output, few-shot), and web-application (API routes, webhooks, background jobs) — against the same AI feature, then produce a cross-layer synthesis. Use when designing or auditing any AI-powered feature. ([kevinvwong/stack-agents](https://github.com/kevinvwong/stack-agents/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/panel-ai-feature.md) · ★1)
- **panel:content**: Run the four GTLI content pipeline agents as a panel — video-script, lesson, assessment, and QA — against the same module specification, then produce a full content package with a QA verdict. Use when generating language learning module content end-to-end. ([kevinvwong/stack-agents](https://github.com/kevinvwong/stack-agents/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/panel-content.md) · ★1)
- **panel:design**: Run the three design discipline agents as a panel — visual-designer, interaction-designer, information-architect — against the same UI or feature, then produce a cross-discipline synthesis surfacing conflicts between visual, behavioral, and structural design decisions. Use for new UI component reviews, information architecture audits, and design system critiques. ([kevinvwong/stack-agents](https://github.com/kevinvwong/stack-agents/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/panel-design.md) · ★1)
- **panel:game**: Run all 6 game design agents as a panel — each reviews the same design artifact or question from their discipline, then produces a cross-discipline synthesis that surfaces conflicts and gaps no single agent would catch alone. Use for GDD reviews, feature design critiques, pre-milestone readiness checks, and design retrospectives. ([kevinvwong/stack-agents](https://github.com/kevinvwong/stack-agents/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/panel-game.md) · ★1)
- **panel:github**: Run all 5 GitHub agents as a panel — each reviews the same repository from their domain (governance, CI/CD, issues, PRs, releases, docs), then produces a cross-domain synthesis that surfaces gaps and conflicts no single agent would catch alone. Use for new repo setup reviews, repo health checks, and pre-open-source checklists. ([kevinvwong/stack-agents](https://github.com/kevinvwong/stack-agents/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/panel-github.md) · ★1)
- **panel:gtli-jgcc**: Run all 11 JGCC (Joan Ganz Cooney Center) learning quality agents as a panel against a GTLI educational product, module, or feature. Covers active learning, engagement, meaningfulness, equity/access, privacy, social/JME, developmental appropriateness, diversity, family/stakeholder, student usability, and RITEC well-being. Any zero on Safety or DEI is a hard fail. ([kevinvwong/stack-agents](https://github.com/kevinvwong/stack-agents/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/panel-gtli-jgcc.md) · ★1)
- **panel:gtli-sim**: Run a simulated user panel across GTLI archetypes — the non-native English learner, the cohort director, the studio coordinator, the administrator, and the prospective student — against a GTLI platform feature. Each archetype reacts to the feature from their lived context; a synthesis identifies moments where the feature serves one persona at the expense of another. ([kevinvwong/stack-agents](https://github.com/kevinvwong/stack-agents/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/panel-gtli-sim.md) · ★1)
- **panel:gtli-ux**: Run all 5 GTLI UX persona agents as a panel — ux-learner, ux-director, ux-coordinator, ux-admin, and ux-synthesis — against the same GTLI platform feature or route, then produce a cross-persona synthesis. Use when auditing the GTLI platform for role-specific UX gaps. ([kevinvwong/stack-agents](https://github.com/kevinvwong/stack-agents/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/panel-gtli-ux.md) · ★1)
- **panel:knowledge**: Run the cross-surface documentation panel — notion-architect + notion-governance + gh-docs — against the same project. Audits how knowledge flows between the Notion workspace and the repo (README, CONTRIBUTING, SECURITY, ADRs, runbooks). Surfaces gaps where a doc lives in one surface but should also live in the other. ([kevinvwong/stack-agents](https://github.com/kevinvwong/stack-agents/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/panel-knowledge.md) · ★1)
- **panel:launch**: Full pre-launch sweep across all disciplines — runs web stack (all 7), quality (web-qa + accessibility + performance), GitHub (repo + actions), and security — then synthesizes into a SHIP / NO-SHIP verdict with a prioritized fix list. Use as the final gate before any production launch. ([kevinvwong/stack-agents](https://github.com/kevinvwong/stack-agents/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/panel-launch.md) · ★1)
- **panel:notion**: Run all 4 Notion specialists as a panel — notion-architect (schema/views), notion-publisher (publish health), notion-importer (read surface), notion-governance (workspace health) — against the same workspace, then synthesize cross-specialty gaps no single agent would catch. Use for workspace setup reviews, migrations from ad-hoc Notion, and quarterly workspace health checks. ([kevinvwong/stack-agents](https://github.com/kevinvwong/stack-agents/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/panel-notion.md) · ★1)
- **panel:onboarding**: Structured intake panel for new projects — convenes product + data + security + infrastructure + gh-repo to produce a single `BLUEPRINT.md` at the target project root before any code is scaffolded. Use this as the thinking artifact that precedes `/setup:project --mode bootstrap`. Each agent contributes a section from their domain; the synthesis pass reconciles conflicts and emits the consolidated blueprint. ([kevinvwong/stack-agents](https://github.com/kevinvwong/stack-agents/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/panel-onboarding.md) · ★1)
- **panel:psych**: Run the two psychology agents as a panel — cognitive-psychologist (working memory, attention, mental models) and behavioral-psychologist (motivation, habit formation, persuasion, dark patterns) — against the same feature or product experience, then produce a synthesis. Use when a feature "feels off," for engagement system reviews, onboarding critiques, and learning product design. ([kevinvwong/stack-agents](https://github.com/kevinvwong/stack-agents/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/panel-psych.md) · ★1)
- **panel:publish**: Run the publishing-readiness panel — product + analytics + notion-publisher — against a PRD or analytics spec. Catches PRDs that aren't testable (product), aren't measurable (analytics), or aren't shaped for the canonical PRDs database (notion-publisher) before they get published. Use as a quality gate before /notion:publish prd or /notion:publish analytics. ([kevinvwong/stack-agents](https://github.com/kevinvwong/stack-agents/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/panel-publish.md) · ★1)
- **panel:quality**: Run the three quality agents as a panel — web-qa (test pyramid), accessibility (WCAG), and performance (Core Web Vitals) — against the same feature or codebase, then produce a cross-discipline synthesis. Use for pre-launch QA gates, new component reviews, and CI pipeline audits. ([kevinvwong/stack-agents](https://github.com/kevinvwong/stack-agents/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/panel-quality.md) · ★1)
- **panel:research**: Run all 4 research agents as a panel — user-research, usability-testing, focus-group, expert-review — against the same product question or feature, then produce a cross-discipline synthesis that maps method gaps and conflicting findings. Use for new feature validation, launch readiness research plans, and when any single research method would leave blind spots. ([kevinvwong/stack-agents](https://github.com/kevinvwong/stack-agents/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/panel-research.md) · ★1)
- **panel:security**: Run three security-focused agents as a panel — web-security (auth/RBAC/rate-limiting), env-debugger (env var leaks/misconfig), and a static analysis pass — against the same codebase or feature, then produce a cross-domain synthesis. Use for pre-launch security gates, new auth feature reviews, and handling a security incident. ([kevinvwong/stack-agents](https://github.com/kevinvwong/stack-agents/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/panel-security.md) · ★1)
- **panel:stack**: Run all 7 web stack agents as a panel — data, security, ai-llm, application, infrastructure, observability, presentation — each reviews the same feature or codebase from their layer, then produces a cross-layer synthesis surfacing integration gaps no single agent catches. Use for new feature reviews, architecture audits, security sweeps, and pre-launch readiness checks. ([kevinvwong/stack-agents](https://github.com/kevinvwong/stack-agents/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/panel-stack.md) · ★1)
- **panel:website**: Run a full website audit panel — website-audit-redesign-planner (structural UX + conversion), gtli-student-lens-audit (persona-specific enrollment funnel), and visual-designer (aesthetic/trust layer) — against a target URL, then synthesize into a prioritized redesign roadmap. Use for pre-launch website reviews, conversion optimization, and brand trust audits. ([kevinvwong/stack-agents](https://github.com/kevinvwong/stack-agents/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/panel-website.md) · ★1)

- **paper-trace**: Trace each final figure and table back to its run, code and inputs, and report any recorded file that has changed since the run. ([akontadakis/paper-skills](https://github.com/akontadakis/paper-skills/blob/HEAD/commands/paper-trace.md))

- **paper-trail-acquire**: Troisième passe du pipeline cible refondu. Lance la cascade PDF (10 sources : Crossref OA + arXiv + OpenAlex + Unpaywall + HAL + CORE + éditeur par DOI + archive.org + WebSearch ; jusqu'à 17 avec les voies par navigateur et les sources étendues opt-in) ciblée sur les refs d'un SOTA donné. Différent de `pipeline run` qui itère sur TOUTES les refs actives — `acquire` ne traite que les refs citées par le SOTA en cours. ([ccfwwm/zerowallscience](https://github.com/ccfwwm/zerowallscience/blob/HEAD/distribution/plugins/dsh-plugin-skills/resources/skills/paper-download/commands/paper-trail-acquire.md) · ★8 · 5 copies)
- **paper-trail-audit-all**: Batch audit of every SOTA in the vault. For each SOTA, identifies refs that are page1_validated (technical OK) but not yet sota_cited_confirmed (content audited), and launches audit-sota on those SOTAs. Returns a per-SOTA summary at the end. ([ccfwwm/zerowallscience](https://github.com/ccfwwm/zerowallscience/blob/HEAD/distribution/plugins/dsh-plugin-skills/resources/skills/paper-download/commands/paper-trail-audit-all.md) · ★8 · 5 copies)
- **paper-trail-audit-article**: Per-citation audit of an academic article (LaTeX or Markdown). Produces RECEIPTS.md classifying each citation as VALID/ADJUST/INVALID/UNVERIFIABLE. Optional --warn inserts inline warnings in a .bak copy of the source. ([ccfwwm/zerowallscience](https://github.com/ccfwwm/zerowallscience/blob/HEAD/distribution/plugins/dsh-plugin-skills/resources/skills/paper-download/commands/paper-trail-audit-article.md) · ★8 · 5 copies)
- **paper-trail-audit-sota**: Audit a SOTA's bibliography. Classify each cited ref by validation state. Optional --purge removes citations to retracted refs from the SOTA (with .bak backup). ([ccfwwm/zerowallscience](https://github.com/ccfwwm/zerowallscience/blob/HEAD/distribution/plugins/dsh-plugin-skills/resources/skills/paper-download/commands/paper-trail-audit-sota.md) · ★8 · 5 copies)
- **paper-trail-cascade**: Acquire PDFs via the 8-source cascade (11 with opt-in extended sources) for a single ref by slug, or a batch filtered by state. Validates page 1 anti-homonymy on each download. ([ccfwwm/zerowallscience](https://github.com/ccfwwm/zerowallscience/blob/HEAD/distribution/plugins/dsh-plugin-skills/resources/skills/paper-download/commands/paper-trail-cascade.md) · ★8 · 5 copies)
- **paper-trail-decide**: Inspect a single reference in detail (identity, PDF status, acquisition history with verdicts, citation context in vault, state transitions) and decide its fate. Offers actionable decisions based on the ref's current state. ([ccfwwm/zerowallscience](https://github.com/ccfwwm/zerowallscience/blob/HEAD/distribution/plugins/dsh-plugin-skills/resources/skills/paper-download/commands/paper-trail-decide.md) · ★8 · 5 copies)
- **paper-trail-doctor**: Audit the registry via the 19 mechanical invariants (I1-I19). Reports ERROR/WARN/INFO violations. Optional auto-fix for safe ones (I4 path, I6 sha256, I9 attempts). ([ccfwwm/zerowallscience](https://github.com/ccfwwm/zerowallscience/blob/HEAD/distribution/plugins/dsh-plugin-skills/resources/skills/paper-download/commands/paper-trail-doctor.md) · ★8 · 5 copies)
- **paper-trail-identify**: Première passe du pipeline cible refondu (read-only). Produit un rapport d'identification : pour chaque mention citée dans le SOTA, indique si la fiche existe déjà au registre, son état actuel, si elle a un PDF, et l'action recommandée (réutiliser / créer / passer en revue). Ne modifie ni le SOTA ni le registre. ([ccfwwm/zerowallscience](https://github.com/ccfwwm/zerowallscience/blob/HEAD/distribution/plugins/dsh-plugin-skills/resources/skills/paper-download/commands/paper-trail-identify.md) · ★8 · 5 copies)
- **paper-trail-ingest**: Ingest the citations of one SOTA into the registry. Extracts bibliographic sections, parses citations via the citation-parser sub-agent, resolves DOI via Crossref/S2, deduplicates against the registry, creates new candidate refs, and substitutes free-text citations with [[wikilinks]]. Required first step to make a legacy SOTA conform to the paper-trail pipeline. ([ccfwwm/zerowallscience](https://github.com/ccfwwm/zerowallscience/blob/HEAD/distribution/plugins/dsh-plugin-skills/resources/skills/paper-download/commands/paper-trail-ingest.md) · ★8 · 5 copies)
- **paper-trail-ingest-all**: Batch INGEST across the entire vault. Scans all SOTAs, identifies those with bibliographic sections containing free-text citations, then runs /paper-trail:ingest on each one sequentially. Reports per-SOTA summary at the end. The single batch entry point to make a legacy vault fully conform to the paper-trail pipeline. ([ccfwwm/zerowallscience](https://github.com/ccfwwm/zerowallscience/blob/HEAD/distribution/plugins/dsh-plugin-skills/resources/skills/paper-download/commands/paper-trail-ingest-all.md) · ★8 · 5 copies)
- **paper-trail-inject-url**: Injecte une URL OA connue (HAL, dépôt uni, NIME, page perso) pour une ref dont la cascade automatique a échoué, puis relance l'acquisition + validation page 1. ([ccfwwm/zerowallscience](https://github.com/ccfwwm/zerowallscience/blob/HEAD/distribution/plugins/dsh-plugin-skills/resources/skills/paper-download/commands/paper-trail-inject-url.md) · ★8 · 5 copies)
- **paper-trail-linkify**: Dernière passe du pipeline cible refondu. Insère les wikilinks finaux dans le SOTA (vers PDF si validé, sinon vers ancre dans une section `## Statut des sources` régénérée idempotemment en bas du fichier). Présuppose que les passes identify/purge/acquire ont été lancées. ([ccfwwm/zerowallscience](https://github.com/ccfwwm/zerowallscience/blob/HEAD/distribution/plugins/dsh-plugin-skills/resources/skills/paper-download/commands/paper-trail-linkify.md) · ★8 · 5 copies)
- **paper-trail-new-paper**: Start writing an academic paper (IMRaD structure) on a topic, with anti-hallucination citation verification at every step. Builds on existing audited SOTAs. ([ccfwwm/zerowallscience](https://github.com/ccfwwm/zerowallscience/blob/HEAD/distribution/plugins/dsh-plugin-skills/resources/skills/paper-download/commands/paper-trail-new-paper.md) · ★8 · 5 copies)
- **paper-trail-new-sota**: Create a new State-of-the-Art review on a topic, guaranteed without hallucinated citations. Inverted workflow (research → acquire PDFs → read → write), refuses to write from memory. ([ccfwwm/zerowallscience](https://github.com/ccfwwm/zerowallscience/blob/HEAD/distribution/plugins/dsh-plugin-skills/resources/skills/paper-download/commands/paper-trail-new-sota.md) · ★8 · 5 copies)
- **paper-trail-purge**: Nettoie les wikilinks invalides d'un SOTA (vers refs retracted, refs `_0000_*` orphelines, suffixes numériques `_2_3_4`, fichiers techniques `20_ATLAS/`, `30_DEV/`, `.canvas`, slugs non-bibliographiques). Opère uniquement sur le SOTA, jamais sur le registre. Backup git auto avant `--apply`. ([ccfwwm/zerowallscience](https://github.com/ccfwwm/zerowallscience/blob/HEAD/distribution/plugins/dsh-plugin-skills/resources/skills/paper-download/commands/paper-trail-purge.md) · ★8 · 5 copies)
- **paper-trail-reactivate-ocr**: Re-evaluate refs in state `awaiting_rtfm_ocr` via `rtfm check`. Transitions to `page1_validated` if OCR succeeded, or `needs_reacquisition` if OCR failed. ([ccfwwm/zerowallscience](https://github.com/ccfwwm/zerowallscience/blob/HEAD/distribution/plugins/dsh-plugin-skills/resources/skills/paper-download/commands/paper-trail-reactivate-ocr.md) · ★8 · 5 copies)

([ccfwwm/zerowallscience](https://github.com/ccfwwm/zerowallscience/blob/HEAD/distribution/plugins/dsh-plugin-skills/resources/skills/paper-download/commands/paper-trail-receipts.md) · ★8 · 5 copies)
- **paper-trail-registry-cleanup**: Nettoyage historique du registre des fiches bibliographiques (les ~900 fiches existantes). Délègue les décisions au sub-agent `textbook-resolver`. Les merge sont propagés automatiquement vers tous les SOTAs via `sota_sync`. ([ccfwwm/zerowallscience](https://github.com/ccfwwm/zerowallscience/blob/HEAD/distribution/plugins/dsh-plugin-skills/resources/skills/paper-download/commands/paper-trail-registry-cleanup.md) · ★8 · 5 copies)

Fully automated cleanup pass after INGEST. ([ccfwwm/zerowallscience](https://github.com/ccfwwm/zerowallscience/blob/HEAD/distribution/plugins/dsh-plugin-skills/resources/skills/paper-download/commands/paper-trail-resolve-textbooks.md) · ★8 · 5 copies)

- **papers-index**: Build/refresh the KB's papers ledger — the one file an agent reads to understand the whole paper corpus without re-reading it. Surfaces the reading backlog (readable PDFs not yet read) and the re-fetch queue (missing/unreadable PDFs). The ledger is the idempotency authority: fetch skips papers already present+readable, summarization skips papers whose PDF is unchanged.
